6.2
Basic Guarantees on Variant Generation and Type Safety for CLC

Next theorems ensures that the SPL typing ensures the coherence of all valid variants.

Until now, we used
Prg
as a mapping so that,
{\mathsf{dom}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}})
denote its domain. We extend that notation to include ground types, namaly we use
{\mathop{\mathsf{dom}\mkern-21.0mu\overset{int}{\phantom{=}}\mkern 9.0mu}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}})
to denote
{{{\mathsf{dom}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}})\cup\{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{int}}}}}},\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{void}}}}}}\ast\}
.

Theorem 6.5

(Safety for annotated struct)
.

Let
{(\Phi,\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}},\textsc{at})
be an SPL with
\Phi=(\mathcal{F},\phi)
such that, both
p
is a valid product and

{{{{{{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{struct}}}}}}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{s}}}}}}\{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{1}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{m}}}}}}_{1}\ldots\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{n}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{m}}}}}}_{n}\}
is in
Prg
.
It holds that:
(i)
{{p\models\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{struct}}}}}}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{s}}}}}})
iff
{{{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{struct}}}}}}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{s}}}}}}\in\mathsf{dom}(\lsem\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}\rsem_{p})
;
and,
(ii) if
{{p\models\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{struct}}}}}}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{s}}}}}})
then:

{{p\models\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{i}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{m}}}}}}_{i})
iff
{{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{i}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{m}}}}}}_{i}
is included in

{{{{\llangle\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{struct}}}}}}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{s}}}}}}\{\overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}}\ \overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{m}}}}}}}\}\rrangle_{p}
;
(iii) if
{\phi\vdash\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}\;\textsc{ok}\
,
{{p\models\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{struct}}}}}}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{s}}}}}})
and
{{p\models\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{i}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{m}}}}}}_{i})
then

{{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{i}\in\mathop{\mathsf{dom}\mkern-21.0mu\overset{int}{\phantom{=}}\mkern 9.0mu}(\lsem\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}\rsem_{p})
,
for all
i
.

Proof.

Point (i) follows because, in accord to the rule
vgList
of the variant generation,
\lsem\_\rsem_{p}
is applied to the list of struct-definitions, and we know that
{{p\models\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{struct}}}}}}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{s}}}}}})
. Likewise,
{{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{i}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{m}}}}}}_{i}
is not removed from
{{{{\llangle\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{struct}}}}}}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{s}}}}}}\{\overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}}\ \overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{m}}}}}}}\}\rrangle_{p}
because the rule
vg2
,
\lsem\_\rsem_{p}
is driven by
{{p\models\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{i}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{m}}}}}}_{i})
: so (ii) is proved.
Point (iii) follows because, in the derivation of
{\phi\vdash\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}\;\textsc{ok}\
is necessarily concluded with the rule
FT-prg
.
In its premises, there is
{{\phi\,\&\&\,\textsc{at}(\overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{SD}}}}}}})\vdash\overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{SD}}}}}}}\;\textsc{ok}\
that includes
{{{{{{\phi\,\&\&\,\textsc{at}(\!\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{struct}}}}}}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{s}}}}}}\!)\vdash\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{struct}}}}}}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{s}}}}}}\{\overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}}\ \overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{m}}}}}}}\}\;\textsc{ok}\

having premises
{{{{{\phi\,\&\&\,\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{struct}}}}}}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{s}}}}}})\models\textsc{at}(\overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}}\ \overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{m}}}}}}})\Rightarrow\textsc{at}(\overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}})
. Therefore, the proof follows from hypotheses on

∎

.

{{{{{{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{f}}}({\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{1}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{x}}}}}}_{1}\ldots\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{n}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{x}}}}}}_{n}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers)\textbraceleft{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{return}}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}};\textbraceright}}}

{{p\models\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{i}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{x}}}}}}_{i})
iff
{{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{i}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{x}}}}}}_{i}
is included (not removed) in

{{{{{{\llangle\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{f}}}({\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{1}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{x}}}}}}_{1}\ldots\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{n}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{x}}}}}}_{n}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers)\textbraceleft{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{return}}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}};\textbraceright}}}\rrangle_{p}

{{p\models(\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}_{1})\&\&\ldots\&\&\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}_{n}))

iff

{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}^{\prime}

.

Proof.

Point (i) and (ii) are similar to that of the
6.5
.
The point (iii) follows by standard (mutual) induction on the (mutual recursive) definition of variant generator.
∎

Let
\Delta
be
{{{{\overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{x}}}}}}}:\overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}}\overset{\text{ with }\textsc{at}(\overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}}\ \overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{x}}}}}}})}{\ }
then,
we denote
{{{{{{\lsem\Delta\rsem_{p}=\{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{x}}}:{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}\mid\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{x}}}:{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}\overset{\text{ with }\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{x}}}}}})}{\ }\in\Delta\text{ , }p\models\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{x}}}}}})\}
, namely the pairs
x
:
T
that are used in the product
p
are available as parameter of the considered function.

Theorem 6.7

(Safety for annotated expression)
.

Let
{(\Phi,\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}},\textsc{at})
be an SPL such that, both
p
is a valid product and
let
{\mathcal{D}^{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}}
be the derivation proving

{\phi\vdash\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}\;\textsc{ok}
.
If
p\models\theta
and
{{\theta;\Delta\vdash\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}:\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}
occurs in
{\mathcal{D}^{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}}

then
{{\lsem\Delta\rsem_{p}\vdash\llangle\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}\rrangle_{p}:\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}
.
Moreover, if
struct

s
is used in the derivation concluding
{{\lsem\Delta\rsem_{p}\vdash\llangle\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}\rrangle_{p}:\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}
then
{p\models\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{s}}}}}})
.

Proof.

If
\mathcal{D}
be the sub-derivation proving
{{\theta;\Delta\vdash\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}:\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}
then, the proof follows by induction on the last rule used in
\mathcal{D}
.
(Indeed, the proof of
{{\lsem\Delta\rsem_{p}\vdash\llangle\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}\rrangle_{p}\vdash:\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}
can be obtained from
{{\theta;\Delta\vdash\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}:\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}

by removing the sub-derivation about the sub-expression which are removed from the VG and, then, erasing the annotating stuff from the remaining derivation.)

•

Cases
FT-int
,
FT-null
,
FT-malloc
can be transformed in typing derivation
for the variant
{\lsem\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}\rsem_{p}
, respectively by using rules
T-int
,
T-null
,
T-malloc
.

•

If the last rule is
FT-par
then, its premises are
{{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{x}}}}}}:\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}\overset{\text{ with }\psi}{\;}\in\Delta

and
\theta\models\psi
. It is easy to see that
\Delta
is predisposed by the (unique) rule
FT-fun
in the ascendants of
\mathcal{D}
in
{\mathcal{D}^{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}}

and, then, no typing judgment for expressions modify it. The rule
FT-fun
put in
\Delta
, for all formal parameter of
f
a pair variable-type together its relative annotation.
So that, the proof follows because
p\models\theta
.

•

Consider the case
FT-app
and let
f
be the applied function.
The premise
{{\theta\models\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{f}}}}}}))
and Lemma
6.6
.i ensures that
the definition of
f
is included in the variant
{\lsem\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}\rsem_{p}
, as required by premises of
T-app
.
Lemma
6.1
ensures that, in the SPL code base
Prg
, the number
of formal parameter of
f
and of arguments supplied in the call is always the same,
thus the premise
{{{\theta\models\textsc{at}(\overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}})\Leftrightarrow\textsc{at}(\overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}}\;\overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{x}}}}}}})

is applied to lists of the same length. This latter condition and Lemma
6.6
.ii ensure that, in the variant
{\llangle\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}\rrangle_{p}
,
the number of (not removed) formal parameters of
f
and the number of (not removed) parameters supplied to it, match exactly.
This match is required implicitly by
T-app
. So, we can easily conclude by induction.

•

Case
FT-member
follows by induction, by observing that
{\theta\models\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{m}}}}}})
ensures that
T

m
is not removed from
{\llangle\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}\rrangle_{p}
,
as required by
T-member
(in the typing derivation for
{\lsem\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}\rsem_{p}
).

•

Case

is similar to the previous one, by replacing it with a

rule in the derivation for
{\lsem\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}\rsem_{p}
.

•

Cases
FT-cond
,
FT-uop
,
FT-bop
and
FT-mfree
follow by induction.

•

Case
FT-seq
. The grammar of the SPL ensures that each expression-list contains at least an expression,
but is the premise
{{\theta\models\exists(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}_{1},\ldots,\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}_{n})
that ensures that in no variant all expressions are removed from the list.
Moreover, the premise
{{{\text{ $\forall i\in\{1....,n\}$, if }(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{i}\neq\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}})\text{ then }(\theta\models\texttt{neverLast}(i,\tilde{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}}))

ensure that expressions ending the list expression in a variant, are always typed with the type of last expression in the CB of the SPL.
Whenever
{p\models\theta\&\&\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}_{i})
holds, by induction on the premise
{{{\theta\&\&\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}_{i});\Delta\vdash\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}_{i}:\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{i}

we have that
{{\lsem\Delta\rsem_{p}\vdash\llangle\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}}}}}_{i}\rrangle_{p}:\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{i}
. Thus we conclude.

∎

We can now prove the type safety result.

Theorem 6.8

(Type safety for CLC)
.

Let
{(\Phi,\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}},\textsc{at})
be an SPL with
\Phi=(\mathcal{F},\phi)
such that
p\models\phi
, viz.
p
is a valid product.
If
{\phi\vdash\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}\;\textsc{ok}
then
{\vdash\lsem\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}\rsem_{p}\;\textsc{ok}
,
viz the variant identified by
p
is well-typed.

Proof.

Let
{\mathcal{D}^{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}}
be the derivation proving
{\phi\vdash\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}\;\textsc{ok}
. This derivation ends with the rule
FT-prg
.
We have to verify that we can build the derivation for the variant
{\lsem\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}\rsem_{p}
that ends with the rule
T-prg
.

•

The premise
Prg

sane
does not immediately imply the sanity of
{\llangle\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}\rrangle_{p}
,
because the first is about the CB of the whole SPL, while the second is about a specific variant where some code fragments has been removed.
Sanity requires 3 conditions. The first requires that for every struct name
s
appearing everywhere in
Prg
we have
{{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{s}}}}}}\in\mathsf{dom}(\overline{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{SD}}}}}}})
.
It follows by Lemmas
6.2
,
6.5
.iii and
6.7
.
The safety for appearing function follows immediately, because the unique typing rule that consider a defined function
f
(out of its definition)
is
FT-app
,
that includes
{{\theta\models\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{f}}}}}}))
in its premises: it ensures that if the application is not removed from a variant
(identified by
p
)
then also the definition of
f
is included. The presence of
f
is ensured by the premise of
FT-prg
.

•

The remaining further premises of
T-prg
are about the function definitions in
{\lsem\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}\rsem_{p}
.
The VG definition ensures that if the definition of
f
is included in the variant then
{{p\models\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{f}}}}}}))
,
so for each function included in
{\lsem\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}\rsem_{p}
, it holds
{{p\models\phi\,\&\&\,\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{f}}}}}}))
.
The rule
FT-prg
includes a premise
{{{{\ \phi\,\&\&\,\textsc{at}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{f}}}}}}))\vdash\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{f}}}}}})\;\textsc{ok}\

for each function
{{{{{{{{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{Prg}}}}}}(\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{f}}}}}})=\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{f}}}({\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{1}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{x}}}}}}_{1}\ldots\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{T}}}}}}_{n}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}\textrm{x}}}}}}_{n}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers)\textbraceleft{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{return}}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_emph}{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}\textrm{e}}};\textbraceright}}}

having a definition in the SPL.
That premise is, in turn, is the conclusion of a rule
FT-fun
that contains sufficient premises to can use the rule
T-fun
for the included function.
In particular, the Lemma
6.7
ensures that we can build the typing derivation for the part (not removed) from expression in the body of
f
.

∎

7
Conclusion and Future Work

In this paper
we presented: (1) the syntax an typing of LC, a core calculus for C programs; (2) the syntax and generation mapping of CLC, a core calculus for SPLs of (L)C; and (3)
a type system for CLC that formalizes a family-based type checking which guarantees that
all the variants of a well-typed CLC SPL are well typed (L)C programs.

In future work we plan to provide a small-step operational semantics for LC, prove its type soundness and to formalize other analyses of C programs and family-based analyises of SPLs of C programs (like, e.g., heap safety).

Moreover, we would like to extend CLC to a larger subset of C and to equip it with a tool chain for analyses of C programs and family-based analyses of SPLs of C programs.
Analyzing C code is notoriously difficult.
Therefore, instead of aiming at capturing the whole C syntax and at providing a tool chain for analyzing existing SPLs of C programs, we aim at providing support for:

•

developing new SPLs of programs written in a subset of C; and

•

refactoring existing SPLs of C programs into SPLs written in a subset of C that is supported by the tool chain.
